**[9370.54s] English:** So at the end of the 90s, Korean Air had more plane crashes than almost any other airline around the globe.

**[9375.50s] English:** Cockpit miscommunication has been a persistent factor in these accidents.

**[9378.50s] English:** For example, the Korean Air Flight 801 crash was attributed to the pilot's decision to land despite the junior officer's disagreement.

**[9385.98s] English:** Evidence of high-power distance, a culture that denotes a heavily hierarchical society.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: hierarchical: 等级制的

**[9393.10s] English:** Gladwell argues that this innate behavior of deep reverence towards elders and superiors highly contributes to cockpit miscommunication, especially on planes designed to be flown by two equals.
